# Graph Report - autonomous-ai-agency  (2026-09-11)

## Corpus Check
- 1501 files · ~2,174,423 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 30407 nodes · 56315 edges · 1285 communities (1114 shown, 115 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 95% EXTRACTED · 5% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2734 edges (avg confidence: 0.91)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `2cbc1cbd`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
